**v427 tests.** Synthetic patterns drawn by the engine from known parameters, with Poisson noise (the scripts are outside the repository): SrTiO₃, rutile, ZnO, LaB₆, Si, NaCl, anatase, corundum, quartz, an orthorhombic, a monoclinic (P2₁/c, β = 110°) and a triclinic cell, YAG (a = 12 Å); Cu Kα₁+₂, Cu Kα₁, Co, Cr and Mo tubes, a synchrotron line (0.8 Å, H 0.003–0.01°) and a high-resolution one (0.41 Å, H about 0.001°); 2θ from 3° to 179°, steps 0.001–0.02°, peaks of 300–20000 counts, crystallites of 1.5 nm to 2 µm; flat, sloping, 1/2θ and amorphous-hump backgrounds; 2–4 seeds per case unless said. For the background, lines the model does not fully fit were added: log-normal crystallites (SrTiO₃ 7 nm, σ 0.5, 10–80°; rutile 10 nm, σ 0.7; ZnO 15 nm, σ 0.6, steps of 0.005°), two populations of one phase (4 and 14 nm, 70:30, SrTiO₃ and rutile) and a WPPM sphere, each refined as one Lorentzian size, at 3000, 30000 and 300000 counts at the peak, with and without a hump (Cu, and ZnO on Mo), 1–2 seeds. A difference step was judged by its Jacobian column against a Richardson extrapolation of central differences at h/8 and h/16 (weighted relative error).

| Constant | Test | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| LAT_RANGE ±10 % | (a) a lone SrTiO₃ whose true cell is 4.5, 5.5 and 7 % over the file's; (b) absent candidates (NaCl, ZnO, anatase beside SrTiO₃; quartz, corundum beside rutile) on a background with an amorphous hump, 2 seeds, and a monoclinic one beside SrTiO₃ (1 seed, at 0.1 only); LAT_RANGE 0.05, 0.1 and 0.2; the reflection list's length | (a) at 0.05 the 5.5 % cell, found by the search, refined onto the bound (a = 4.1003 Å against 4.1198, χ²_ν 7.7); at 0.1 and 0.2 it came to 4.11975 (χ²_ν 1.02). 7 % is not found at any value: the search reaches ±6 % for a lone phase, (1.03)² − 1 = 6.1 % over the two rounds of several. (b) every absent candidate left out at every value, the present phase's size unchanged. At 0.2 the list is 1.3–1.4 times longer (SrTiO₃ 24 → 33 reflections, YAG 153 → 219, the monoclinic cell 755 → 979) | kept: it must hold what the cell search reaches, with room for the refinement beyond; 5 % is under that, 20 % changes no result |
| Reflection-list margin | — | — | already v426 |
| θ_lim = (2θ_end + 2°)/2 | patterns drawn with every line to 179° and cut at 40°, 60° and 90°; SrTiO₃ and rutile of 2, 4 and 10 nm; refined with the list as it is (4–26 reflections) and with every reflection (33–68), 3 seeds | size, strain, cell, ΔB and scale within 0.15 esd of each other in all 18 cases (within 0.04 in 16; the 0.1–0.15 for 2–4 nm lines, several degrees wide, just past a 40° or 90° end): the list already reaches 0.9 of the end's d (about 6° beyond a 40° end), and the far tails of the lines past it go into the background | kept |
| Cell-angle bounds ±15° | a monoclinic phase (P2₁/c) whose true β is 0.5, 1, 2, 3 and 6° below the file's 110° (the cell search scales the lengths only); the reflection list at ±5°, ±15°, ±30° | found to 2° (β = 107.999(2)° for 108°); from 3° off the refinement stops in a false minimum near the file's β (110.24°, χ²_ν 40; 109.32°, χ²_ν 59), inside the box: the bound never acts. ±15° moves the d of a (101)-type plane by +12/−14 % (d = a·cos(β/2) for a = c), the scale of the lengths' ±10 %. The list: monoclinic 556, 755, 1098 reflections, triclinic 835, 1435, 2480 (604 at the file's cell); lines beyond the end are drawn only once the cell brings them in | kept: the same reach in d as the lengths' box; what limits an angle is the start (§3.2.8), not the bound |
| Lattice difference steps 10⁻⁶·v₀ Å, 10⁻⁵° | columns of a, b, c, β: 14 cases, cells 3–12 Å, H 0.001–1°, Lorentzian and WPPM | within 2.5·10⁻⁶ on laboratory patterns; 2·10⁻⁴ (Si to 150° at 0.8 Å) and 5.4·10⁻⁴ (lines 0.002° wide) on synchrotron lines: an esd moves by as much, a converged value not at all | kept |
| Default profile starts | autoRefine from the defaults and from W 0.01, Y 0.1, U 0.01, X 0.02 on 10 cases (Cu and Mo standards, Lorentzian, log-normal, Stephens strain, faults, B per site, texture, orthorhombic, triclinic), 2 seeds | identical results (largest difference 0 esd): autoRefine sets every start | kept |
| zero [−2, 2]°, step 10⁻⁵° | column; the standard's zero scan covers ±0.3° | column within 5·10⁻⁸ (laboratory) and 7·10⁻⁵ (H 0.001°); the bound is never approached by a zero the scan found | kept |
| disp [−3, 3]°, step 10⁻⁵° | as the zero | column within 5·10⁻⁸ and 7·10⁻⁵; ±3° is ±6.3 mm at 240 mm, ±2.6 mm at 100 mm | kept |
| U, V, W bounds; step 10⁻⁶ deg² | columns from laboratory to high-resolution synchrotron lines | no instrument nears the bounds. The step was three times W on the high-resolution beamline (W = 3·10⁻⁷ deg²): W's column 46 % off, V's 4 %. Now at most 10⁻³ of the smallest H_G² over the pattern (÷tan²θ for U, ÷tanθ for V): every column within 2.5·10⁻⁸ there, and laboratory patterns keep 10⁻⁶ (columns within 4·10⁻⁵, unchanged). Points where H_G² is on its floor do not count, and with all of them there the step stays 10⁻⁶: a sample refined without the standard's profile (SrTiO₃ 7 nm, Si 200 nm, true Gaussian 0) takes W to 0, where a step of 10⁻¹¹ (the formula on the floor) gave W a zero column, dropped it from the covariance (Y's esd 0.0054 against v426's 0.0072) and kept it at 0; with the rule, the values and esds are v426's | bounds kept; step derived |
| X, Y [0, 10]°, step 10⁻⁶ | columns | within 4.8·10⁻⁶ everywhere (3·10⁻⁷ on synchrotron lines); 0 is physical, 10° a guard no instrument nears | kept |
| asym [0, 0.2], step 10⁻⁴ | column | within 1.3·10⁻⁴ on laboratory patterns; 0.4–1 % at A = 0.001–0.002 on synchrotron lines (the reference itself uncertain at 10⁻³); 0.2 is a goniometer without Soller slits | kept |
| Background start | as the default profile starts | solved linearly before any step: no effect | kept |
| Background order T₀…T₆ | direct refinements at N = 2–16 (10 cases, 4 seeds); autoRefine on 17 cases, 2 seeds (flat, sloping, 1/2θ and one- or two-hump backgrounds; SrTiO₃, rutile, YAG, a monoclinic phase, mixtures, absent candidates). Then a degree chosen at each pass by BIC on the scales and background (from 6 up to one term per 0.04 Å⁻¹ of d\*, at most 40; stepwise while one or two more terms lower B by 10), against the misfit patterns (log-normal, two populations, WPPM sphere; 3000–300000 counts) with and without a hump; two variants of it: cosines cos(kπ·(2θ − 2θ₀)/span) in place of the terms beyond T₆, and beside each phase a copy of it with lines 4 (also 2.5, 6) times broader, solved with the background | T₀…T₆ leaves a hump (FWHM 12° at 26°, Cu, 15–120°; two humps over 10–90°) to the phases: sizes 1.6–10 % off (a 10 nm rutile with exact Lorentzian lines −5.9 %, 23 esd at 30000 counts; ZnO on Mo +4 %), cells up to 6 esd, χ²_ν 1.45–2.5, a 5 wt % rutile 44 % too small. The BIC choice takes the hump (sizes and cells within 2.8 esd), but on a smooth background it also took the lines' own tails where their shape is not fully modelled: log-normal SrTiO₃ and two populations went to the largest degree (18 over 10–80°) from 30000 counts (two populations in one seed of two already at 3000), ZnO to 15–19 from 30000, two-population rutile to 9–12; the background then lay 0.8–3.7 % of the peak off the true one (T₀…T₆: 0.2–1.4 %) and the size 1.3–11 % off T₀…T₆'s (up to 26 esd). Two seeds at 3000 counts read 7.30 and 6.81 nm (T₀…T₆: 6.63 and 6.70): the noise draw decided the degree, and the size with it. Every term from T₇ to T₁₈ moved the background most at an end of the range, where a Chebyshev term of degree N resolves span·π²/(4N²) (0.5° for N = 18 over 70°). With cosines and broad copies the smooth backgrounds stayed at 6 up to 30000 counts (ZnO at 300000 still 18, −3.4 %) and the humps were still taken (sizes within 1.7 %, z ≤ 2.6), but with a hump and a misfit together the degree rose and the size moved 2.1–12 % from its value without the hump (T₀…T₆: −5.9 to +4.1 %; the copies alone, 2.6–6.5 %; the first attempt, within 0.8 %, its degree high with or without the hump); a 3 nm minority phase on a hump was lost in both seeds. The WPPM sphere and log-normal rutile kept 6 | kept: T₀…T₆, as in v426 (the first v427 attempt's BIC choice, on the card and in the CSV, removed). The hump bias stays, documented (RIETVELD.md §3.9); a separate term for a hump, much broader than the lines, is the way left open |
| 1/2θ term below 20° | autoRefine on rutile from 5, 10, 15, 20, 25 and 30° to 110°, with and without air scatter (1600/2θ on 80 counts), v426 against v427, 2 seeds; the term's pivot among T₀…T_N | refined values within 0.24 esd of v426's in all 24 runs, unchanged where the term is kept. The BIC keeps the term from 5° with air scatter (from 10° in one seed of two) and drops it from 15° on, where its pivot among T₀…T₆ is 5·10⁻⁵ and under 10⁻⁸ from T₁₂ (the collinearity that made N = 16 crawl). Since choosing it moves nothing beyond 0.24 esd, and the choice went with the degree's (removed, row above) | kept: the start rule (below 20°, never at 2θ ≤ 0) |
| Phase scale start 10⁻³, [0, ∞) | as the default profile starts | solved linearly first: no effect; ≥ 0 is physical | kept |
| ΔB [−2, 10] Å², step 10⁻⁴ | column | within 3·10⁻⁹. At its bounds exp(−2ΔB s²) scales the 150° intensity (Cu) by e^1.6 and e^−7.8: a value there is an absorption or roughness misfit, and is listed atBound | kept |
| X_s, Y_s [0, 10]°, step 10⁻⁶ | columns; the size the bound means for each anode | columns within 5·10⁻⁶. X_s's 10° is ε = 4.4 % whatever λ. Y_s's 10° meant 0.79 nm at Cu, 0.37 nm at Mo, 0.92 nm at Co and 1.18 nm at Cr, so "its size broadening runs to its bound" (RIETVELD.md §6.4) meant another size with each anode | X_s kept; Y_s derived: K·λ₁/0.8 nm (9.9° Cu, 4.6° Mo, 14.8° Cr) |
| March–Dollase r [0.2, 5], step 10⁻⁴ | column; autoRefine on ZnO with a (001) texture of r = 0.3, 0.4, 0.5 and 3 | column within 6·10⁻⁸. r = 0.4, 0.5 and 3 recovered (0.4012(8), 0.501(6·10⁻⁴), 2.989(6)). r = 0.3 was not: the stages before the texture's run at r = 1, the size runs to its bound and r to 0.2; a direct refinement from r = 0.8 reaches 0.3035(14). That is the order of the stages (§3.2.8), not the bound: r = 0.2 already makes (00l) 125 times stronger and (hk0) 11 times weaker | kept |
| Log-normal σ [0, 1.5], step 10⁻⁴ | column; autoRefine (starts 0.3 and 0.6) on SrTiO₃ with σ = 0.15 and 1.1 | column within 3·10⁻⁶; σ = 0.15 came out 0.141(4), σ = 1.1 came out 1.094(6). At 1.5 the volume-weighted size is e^7.9 times the median | kept |

**v427 tests.** As for §3.2.6. A pull is (fit − truth)/esd.

| Constant | Test | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| Cholesky pivot tolerance 10⁻¹⁰ | 1 − R² of each determined parameter (from the correlation matrix) in 7 refinements; exactly collinear pairs | exactly collinear: 0 (X with X_s, reported tied); the smallest among determined parameters 2·10⁻⁸ (Y with Y_s, told apart only by Kα₂'s λ₂/λ₁) and 6·10⁻⁶ (the zero of LaB₆ to 60° with the cell and displacement free), the others ≥ 0.06 | kept |
| solveLinear: pivot 10⁻¹² (v426 replaced the four passes by NNLS) | χ² against an independent solve (Gram–Schmidt QR, reorthogonalised) on T₀…T_N with and without 1/2θ, N up to 30, three ranges | equal to 10⁻¹³ wherever the 1/2θ term's pivot is above 10⁻¹²; at 3·10⁻¹⁴ (15–120°, T₀…T₂₄) the term is dropped, 0.5 of χ² (10⁻⁴ relative) above the QR's: a fit of noise | kept |
| Marquardt λ₀ 10⁻³, ×10, ÷10; floor 10⁻⁹ | λ₀ = 10⁻⁴, 10⁻³ and 10⁻² on 7 refinements; the floor on T₀…T₁₆ (a bgDegree of 16) with the 1/2θ term, from a start off the truth, cap 80 | the same minima at every λ₀ but one short-range LaB₆, where 10⁻⁴ stopped at χ²_ν 0.982 against 0.969. With the floor at 10⁻⁹, a direction whose pivot lies between 10⁻¹¹ and 10⁻⁹ took 1–50 % of its step per iteration: 80 iterations, unconverged (χ²_ν 1.039 in the last iterations, 1.036696 after the final linear re-solve), where a floor of 10⁻¹¹ converges in 12–13 (1.036659); well-conditioned refinements are unchanged | λ₀ and factors kept; floor derived: a tenth of the pivot tolerance |
| Tries per iteration 12 | the λ the tries reach | from the floor of 10⁻⁹ they reached 10³; from 10⁻¹¹ they would reach 10. A correlation-scaled matrix of k parameters has no eigenvalue above k, so from λ = 10k a step is a short one down the gradient | derived: 12, and on until λ ≥ 10k (at most 30) |
| Iteration limits 40, 60, 4 | iterations per stage of autoRefine on the 10 cases of the default-start test and the 17 background cases (these with backgrounds of up to 23 terms), 2 seeds | 2–9 per stage and pass, 12–14 for the orthorhombic cell's lattice stage; none reached 40 | kept |
| Convergence: shifts under 1 % of the esds, or Δχ² under 10⁻⁹ | 40 seeds of a monoclinic refinement with the rule and with 10⁻⁴ of the esds and 10⁻¹³ | every pull the same to the digits shown (3.2 against 4.2 iterations) | kept |
| Linear re-solves | — | tested-synthetic in v425; v426 resets λ (§2.4.2) | kept |
| Covariance scaling (JᵀWJ)⁻¹·χ²_ν | Monte Carlo of 40 seeds each: SrTiO₃ at 300 and at 20000 counts, rutile with ΔB, a monoclinic cell (80 seeds) | rms pulls 0.84–1.18 (cell, displacement, size, strain, ΔB, scale; 40 seeds give ±0.11). The monoclinic a has a mean pull of −0.42 over 80 seeds (sd 1.05), the other parameters within ±0.3; v426 gives the same pulls to the digits: a small offset of the fit, not of the scaling | kept |
| χ²_ν floors elsewhere | read against their use | the decisions (a phase put back by ΔBIC, the shape's ΔBIC, its early rejection and polish) divide by max(1, χ²_ν): a χ²_ν under 1 does not inflate a gain. The esds of whole-pattern fits scale by χ²_ν with no floor (refine, the angular profiles since v426, ownEvidence): with N − P in the thousands χ²_ν is known to ±2 %. The Williamson–Hall line (3–15 points) only inflates, as the Birge ratio is used for few points | kept |
| Parameters at a bound | — | tested-synthetic in v425 | kept |
| TIE 10⁻⁹ | Rwp along the cell search for a present phase and an absent one; the rounding of Rwp | rounding 2·10⁻¹⁶; exact ties (7 points) on the absent phase's curve, where its scale solves to 0; the smallest real difference between neighbouring points 2.5·10⁻⁷ (absent), 10⁻³ (present). 10⁻⁹ separates rounding from any real difference. (That the absent phase's nearly flat curve, at the noise level, still has a minimum is the cell search's rule, §3.1, not this tolerance) | kept |

**v427 tests.** Synthetic patterns with Poisson noise and a known truth, refined by `autoRefine` (scripts in the v427 B5 scratch folder). Structures: SrTiO₃, corundum, ZnO, anatase, rutile, Si, LaB₆, NaCl, quartz, Mg, YAG, a Pnma, a monoclinic and a triclinic cell. Radiation: Cu Kα₁/Kα₂, Kα₁ alone, Co and Mo. Instrumental profiles: laboratory, high-resolution, broad, Gaussian, Lorentzian, and a synchrotron-like one a tenth of the laboratory's. Steps 0.003–0.03°, sizes 1.5–1000 nm, 1–2 seeds. Each constant was varied through a copy of the engine that reads it from the environment. A second agent then reviewed the changed rows on cases the first had not used (the "Review" parts of the rows), against the v426 engine run on the same patterns.

All 9 broad fits end at the truth's χ² (D 1.50–3.02 nm for 1.5–3). A window of ±3 or ±4 FWHM reads isolated lines at 0.89–0.97 but merged groups at 1.5–2.5 | kept: a start only, measured harmless |

Review, at ±3 % and ±5 %, 2 seeds: present phases 4–4.5 % off their files' cells (rutile 20 wt % beside SrTiO₃, anatase 60 wt % beside ZnO, quartz 5 wt % beside Si) were lost in all 6 runs at ±3 % (the anatase's 60 % given to ZnO) and are found at ±5 %, within 0.12 wt %; absent candidates 4–4.5 % off a present phase (copies of SrTiO₃, corundum and ZnO beside two populations, a size distribution or a clean host; NaCl, whose cell at f = 0.963 puts its (220), (311), (400) on Si's; 14 runs) give v426's verdicts in all 14: left out in 9; in 5, beside a broad host whose lines had brought the copy within reach at ±3 % already, kept by both (in 4 beside the host, every line shared, with the warning that neither can be told; in 1 in its place) | changed: ±5 % (a lone phase then ±10 %, the lattice bound) |

A zero 0.03° off: 0.6–1.7·10⁻⁴ (v426 1.1–10·10⁻⁴). The zero freed as well: its esd 0.06–0.11°, the cell's 10–12× larger (zero, displacement and asymmetry are hardly told apart). Below 3 both fail. #### 3.2.9 Phase detection (v425 rules) (14)

| Constant | Value | Where (file:line, v425) | Role | Origin | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Significance | scale > 3 esd, after the first stage and at the end of each pass | xrd-rietveld.js:2422 | first leave-out test | convention | 3σ. Weak for very broad phases: 2 nm rutile at 8 wt % has 1.9–3.0 σ while Δχ² = 24–37 (§2.5). |
| Plausibility: cell on its bound | a, b or c within 1e-9 (relative) of the ±10 % bound | xrd-rietveld.js:2447, 2449 | second leave-out test (several phases present) | tested-synthetic | New in v425: catches an absent NaCl that only fits a hump (its a walked to +10.0 %). |
| Plausibility: broadening on its bound | Ys or Xs at its upper bound (10°), with a standard's profile | xrd-rietveld.js:2451 | second leave-out test (several phases present) | tested-synthetic | New in v425: absent quartz beside a hump (Ys = 10°) and on 37D; absent rutile on 37D (Xs). Without a standard it is not applied. |
| Plausibility: crystallites under two cells | D < max(1 nm, 2 × the longest cell edge), with a standard's profile | xrd-rietveld.js:2454 | second leave-out test (several phases present) | tested-synthetic | New in v425. The 3 nm NaCl on 37D passes it; the 1 nm floor and the factor 2 are choices. |
| Clear line: reach | ±1.5 FWHM (CLEAR_FWHM), Kα2 included | xrd-rietveld.js:2197 | a line is clear when no rival line overlaps this reach | tested-synthetic | New in v425 (replaces the "≥ 2/3 of the Bragg intensity" region). Set on synthetic 7 nm SrTiO3 + rutile and absent NaCl; checked on 33A/37D. |
| Clear line: rival height | rival lines at least 1/10 as high (CLEAR_HEIGHT) | xrd-rietveld.js:2197 | which rival lines count | tested-synthetic | New in v425; same tests. |
| Lines counted | above 2 % of the phase's strongest line | xrd-rietveld.js:2221 | denominator of "alone" | arbitrary | Carried over from v424 (then: points above 2 % of the phase's own calculated maximum). |
| Points for α | more than 10 marked points | xrd-rietveld.js:2237 | α computed only above it | arbitrary | Not in RIETVELD.md §6.4; when it fails the warning says no line is clear (§2.5). |
| Local background in the α fit | a straight line c₀ + c₁(2θ − 2θ̄) per stretch of marked points | xrd-rietveld.js:2238 | takes up background and rival tails | arbitrary | v424 had one line for all points. |
| α esd | scaled by √χ²_ν (floor 1e-12) | xrd-rietveld.js:2252 | esd of α | convention | As refine(). |
| Own evidence | alone ≥ 5 % and α > 3 esd | xrd-rietveld.js:2255 | third leave-out test | tuned-on-user-data | Set in v419–v424 on the user's SrTiO3 with absent NaCl and synthetic rutile; kept with the v425 clear-line region. When no phase has a clear line, none is dropped (warning). |
| Drop order | one phase per pass: least "alone", then most "under" | xrd-rietveld.js:2551 | restart rule | arbitrary | Order of removal with several weak phases. |
| Shared-peak warning | α < 0.5 | xrd-rietveld.js:2574 | warns that a fraction rests on shared peaks | arbitrary | Warning only. |
| Twins | same lattice type and atom count, cell mass within 0.1 %, a, b, c within 2 % | xrd-rietveld.js:2196 | two copies of a CIF count as one phase | arbitrary | Allows two size populations of one phase. |

**v427 tests.** A present/absent matrix of synthetic samples built on general structures, not on the user's (SrTiO₃ appears only as one host among many), 71 samples, 2 seeds each: 14 with present minorities of 1–5 wt % (eleven structure pairs; Cu, Co and Mo); 19 with an absent candidate beside a host the plain model cannot fit (a size distribution, anisotropic strain, two populations fitted as one, an amorphous hump, texture), 7 of them with the candidate's lines on the host's, and 6 more with absent candidates of many lines (monoclinic, triclinic, YAG, Pnma); 7 hard pairs whose lines all overlap (Mg and ZnO, cells 1 % apart; a perovskite 2.3 % larger beside SrTiO₃; two corundums 2.5 % apart); 4 twin cases; 15 crowded mixtures of broad lines (two or three phases of 6–30 nm, 5–60 wt %, five of them with an absent candidate); 6 with several weak or absent candidates at once. Laboratory profile, 15–120°. Every own-intensity evaluation was recorded (each phase's share in clear lines, α and its esd) under several settings at once, so the thresholds could be scanned without refitting. The review ran the matrix's absent-candidate samples again at 60 000 counts at the top and its crowded mixtures at 600 (6000 above), one seed, recording the put-back's two tests.

| Constant | Test | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| Significance | — | — | already v426 (a phase it leaves out goes back above dP·ln N + 10) |
| Plausibility: cell on its bound | not this group's (tested-synthetic in v425); seen through the matrix | left out an absent quartz beside two corundum populations (2 of 2 runs); no present phase | kept |
| Plausibility: broadening on its bound | as above | left out 5 absent candidates (quartz beside a hump, NaCl beside two Si populations, Si beside a size distribution); no present phase | kept |
| Plausibility: crystallites under two cells | as above | not reached; present phases of 4–6 nm stand well above it (two cells 0.8–2.6 nm) | kept |
| Clear line: reach | as above; a reach of 1.0 FWHM on 18 hard and absent-candidate samples (v426 engine) | the same verdicts but one: the 2 wt % perovskite beside SrTiO₃ found with 1.0 (not with 1.5) | kept: tested on synthetic data in v425; one seed on 18 cases is too little to change it (a narrower reach also clears an absent candidate's lines nearer a host's tails) |
| Clear line: rival height | as above | — | kept |
| Lines counted | 0.5, 2, 5, 10 % of the strongest, at every recorded evaluation (266 phase evaluations); α of present minorities (1–3 wt %, 7 samples × 6 seeds) at 0.5, 2, 5 % | at 0.5 % an absent monoclinic candidate beside two corundum populations passes (α/esd 12, 2 of 2 runs: its weak lines take up the host's misfit); at 5 and 10 % no verdict changes; present α 1.008 ± 0.047 at each | kept: weak lines carry little of a present phase's α and much of an absent one's noise |
| Points for α | — | — | already v426 (no limit) |
| Local background in the α fit | none, a constant, a straight line, a quadratic per stretch: the same records, and α of the 42 present minorities | absent candidates at their clear lines (10 evaluations): none passes with the line (≤ 0.6 esd) or the quadratic; with none, the absent monoclinic passes at 7–13 esd, with a constant at 4–9 esd. Present: α 0.996, 1.007, 1.008, 1.011, sd 0.029, 0.047, 0.047, 0.138; the quadratic loses a 3 wt % corundum beside YAG (α/esd 6.6 → 2.7) | kept: the least that takes up the host's tails and the background, which an absent candidate's α otherwise reads |
| α esd | the code against its definition; (α − 1)/esd over the 42 present minorities | scaled by the refinement's √χ²_ν, as `refine`'s esds; rms 0.59: conservative, since the phase's scale was fitted to the same points | kept: convention, on the safe side |
| Own evidence (§3.1) | the share in clear lines A and α/esd Z scanned over every recorded evaluation (A 0–0.2, Z 2–8); then the drops it made, by what leaving the phase out cost (χ² without it against with it) | no absent candidate passes at any A ≥ 0 and Z ≥ 2 (18 evaluations reached the test; the others went by the earlier tests); of 194 present phases at their first evaluation 31 fail it at A = 0 and 40 at 0.05 (Z 3), all in crowded or overlapping patterns. In v426 such a phase stayed out: a present phase of 30–50 wt % lost in all 7 runs of four crowded samples (χ²_ν 37–313). Leaving out a present phase there multiplied χ² by 2.8–300, the others taking up 24–53 % of its Σ w·y_p²; an absent one raised χ² 0.4–10 %, or ×3.1–3.9 with 87 % taken up where it had held a host's second population | kept 5 % and 3 esd; added: a phase it leaves out goes back when χ² more than doubles without it and the rise exceeds a quarter of its Σ w·y_p² (the geometric middle of 13 % and 47 %). The 12 crowded samples (24 runs) now keep every phase, fractions within 0.25 wt %; the absent-candidate verdicts unchanged; 2–3 wt % minorities whose lines all overlap a host's still go (χ² 5–29 % without them). Review, the matrix at other counts (1 seed): at ten times the counts (60 000 at the top) all 34 absent candidates of 33 samples still go, the χ² ratios of those the own-intensity test left out 1.006–1.16 (a share above a quarter) or 1.10–4.3 (a share of 0.09–0.13); at 600 counts 9 of the 11 present phases it left out in the crowded samples come back (×2.3–30), 2 stay out as in v426 (rutile 10 wt % beside 8 nm anatase ×1.27; corundum 25 wt % beside 10 nm YAG and ZnO ×1.75): the doubling is a ratio of χ² totals, and where noise is most of χ² a phase must add more than it. The two tests do different work: the share separates only the candidates that held an isostructural host's second population (0.13); most of those that took a small misfit leave shares of 0.28–0.95 and are kept out by the doubling |
| Drop order | samples where two phases fail the own-intensity test at once (crowded mixtures; NaCl and LaB₆ beside a size distribution; Mg 3 % and an absent corundum beside ZnO; quartz and a monoclinic cell beside two corundum populations) | two phases failed it in the same pass only in crowded mixtures where both were present (14 evaluations): the χ² rule then brings back each in turn, so the order changes only which goes first. In the 6 samples built for it the candidates went by different tests one after the other (3 esd, cell bound, own intensity), all absent ones out, the 3 wt % Mg beside ZnO lost as in the hard pairs | kept |
| Shared-peak warning | α at the final fits | no present phase with own evidence below α 0.7 in the matrix; in v426 it fired on a corundum whose fraction was 58 wt % against 34 (a rutile lost beside it) | kept: display; it flags a fraction that rests on shared peaks |
| Twins | the cell tolerance at 1 % and 3 % against 2 %, on twin and near-twin cases (copies 1–2.5 % apart, present or absent, beside one or two populations) | 1 %: a perovskite copy 1.5 % larger at 2 wt % beside SrTiO₃ is lost (2 of 2; χ²_ν 1.0 → 1.17). 3 %: one 2.3 % larger at 2 wt % is found (2 of 2, lost at 2 %), but an absent corundum copy 2.5 % larger is kept at 1.9 wt % beside a corundum with a size distribution (1 of 2). The other 14 runs alike | kept: between the 1.5 % a copy must be allowed and the 2.5 % at which an absent copy takes the host's tails; two copies of one file have equal cells whatever the tolerance |

**v427 tests.** Synthetic patterns drawn by the engine itself with Poisson noise, on two generic instruments (a Cu Bragg–Brentano and a synchrotron-like one), none the user's: plates, discs, needles, rods and boxes of SrTiO₃, rutile, ZnO, corundum, an orthorhombic (Pnma), a monoclinic and a triclinic phase, aspects 1.3 to 50, in general orientations and on lattice directions, 1376–6001 points; spheres for the null cases. The search was run as the code runs it, with one constant changed at a time, and its end compared with the lowest minimum known (a refinement from the truth, or a lower end of the search). Scripts `s1`, `s2`, `s4`, `s6`, `s7`, `t1`, `u3` in the scratchpad's `v427/B6`.

| Constant | Test | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| SHAPE_SOFT | — | already v426 (sin 1°, applied after the widths, §2.6.1) | already-v426 |
| SHAPE_STARTS, SHAPE_FULL | not retested (tested-synthetic) | The 11 starts are the sphere-like one and a plate and a needle per low-index family: a tetragonal or lower phase has 5 families and no random start, a hexagonal or trigonal one 2, a cubic one 4. Every search below, 13 bodies in every crystal family, ended within 0.04 χ²_ν of the lowest minimum known, or held the same frame with the same sizes | kept |
| Brief trials | not retested (tested-synthetic) | — | kept |
| Early rejection | 16 isotropic patterns fitted with a cylinder, an ellipsoid, a spheroid or a box, on 4 structures and steps 0.015–0.06° | All 16 rejected early, ΔBIC +3 to +22; two run to the end anyway were rejected there too (+6.8, +6.9). v426 made it the held route's (§2.6.2) | kept |
| Polish | Off, 4 turns without the stop, turns of 0.005 and 0.08 rad: 6 bodies in general orientations (off alone on 2 more) and 4 on lattice directions (a 50:1 disc on [111], a box on [110], a ZnO disc on [001], a rutile rod on [001]) | Every variant ended at the same minimum, or held the same frame with the same sizes: within 0.04 χ²_ν off the lattice. On it, the free fit before holding (whose angles and sizes enter a held result's esds) varied by up to 0.55 χ²_ν, the polish ahead of none: the rutile rod's 0.20 χ²_ν above the lowest with it, 0.28 without. It costs 4–14 % of the evaluations; 4 turns cost 10–35 % more for nothing | kept |
| Shape acceptance | The 16 null patterns; near the limit, a 12 × 9 nm SrTiO₃ spheroid (3 draws) and a 12 × 8 nm rutile cylinder (2) | No null kept. The spheroid rejected (two early, one held at ΔBIC −3.5), the cylinder kept (−606, −580). ΔBIC = Δχ²/max(1, χ²_ν,iso) + ΔP·ln N against −10 is Kass and Raftery's "very strong" | kept |
| Held-on-lattice test | Truths on lattice frames and turned 2–8° off them: SrTiO₃ cylinders and boxes, thin and thick, rutile rods, ZnO plates, Pnma boxes, 50:1 discs on [111] (46 refinements) | Δχ² was divided by the isotropic fit's χ²_ν, which the shape's own misfit inflates (3.1–3.4 for a 16 × 12 × 2.4 nm box, 9–16 for thinner bodies): that box 4° off the frame was held in 3 draws of 3, at Δχ² 10–23 in the free fit's noise. Now the free fit's own χ²_ν (floored at 1): free at 4° and 8°, and all 29 on-lattice refinements held as before (Δχ² ≤ 4.3). A 50:1 disc 2° off is held (Δχ² 1.3–6.1, the free axis 1.6–2.1° off), 5° off free. The 95 % quantiles kept. Review: 29 more on-lattice fits, SrTiO₃ boxes (13 draws, 3 of them with the instrument profile given wrong or a Gaussian strain left out of the model), corundum 24 × 18 × 5 nm and ZnO boxes on [001] (6 each), SrTiO₃ ellipsoids 20 × 12 × 4 nm (4): all held, Δχ² 0–7.1 (the two highest with the wrong instrument); a corundum box 4° off free in 3 draws of 3 (Δχ² 15–36) | changed |
| BIC sample size | A null cylinder and the 12 × 9 nm spheroid at steps 0.015, 0.03 and 0.06° (N 6001, 3001, 1501), the counts per point scaled so that the total stays the same | ΔBIC of the nulls 6.5–7.0, 3.0–5.8, 4.4–5.7; of the spheroid 1.5–1.7, 1.6–2.3, 0.8–3.7: halving the step adds (dP − n_R)·ln 2 ≈ 0.7 to the early route, small beside the margin of 10, and a real shape's Δχ² does not change with the step at equal counts | kept |

Tilted 0.02 or 0.08 rad, it reached the truth | kept |

**v427 tests.** Refinements started from the truth (shape and orientation free) on synthetic patterns, read by `bodyShapeOf`; the instruments and structures of §3.2.10, none of them the user's. The pairs test on plates 150 × 150 × 4 nm, two equal dimensions near the resolution and one thin, SrTiO₃ at 20 000 counts: 16 boxes, 8 ellipsoids, 8 elliptic cylinders. Angular esds against the truth on SrTiO₃ discs, rutile rods and SrTiO₃ boxes in random orientations. Display rules on set widths; the lattice-direction rules on 12 structures of every crystal family, 728 directions each. Scripts `s3`, `s3b`, `s5`, `s8`, `u1`, `u2` in the scratchpad's `v427/B6`. Review (`v427/B6/rev`, `r1`, `r3`, `r4`, `r5`, `t1`): pairs of equal widths in random orientations at 6000 counts, Cu, 3001 points, on SrTiO₃, rutile, ZnO, corundum and the Pnma phase — ellipsoids 12 × 12 × 4 and 10 × 10 × 3 nm and needles 5 × 5 × 20 nm, elliptic cylinders 12 × 12 × 4 and 10 × 10 × 3 nm, boxes 12 × 12 × 3 and 5 × 5 × 20 nm; 10–29 draws each, about 300 refinements in all.

| Constant | Test | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| Bound rule in zr | The 32 plates; a spheroid plate 3 nm thick with an unbounded equator; a 4 nm disc with an unbounded diameter. Review: the pairs of equal widths on five structures above | An in-plane width fell on 0 in 9 of 32, and v426 told all 9 apart from the equal other (z 2.6–5.7). A width on or near 0 has a linear esd of 0.03–0.05° on a box face (beside 0.02° for the others: a face acts to first order), but 0.2–840° on a ball's axis or an elliptic section's (second order: a 150 nm axis at 0.0005° read ±0.9°, 2.2σ from a 4 nm thickness). The other agent profiled such pairs where the smaller width's esd exceeds it: one common width, the rest refined again (4 iterations) at three points and the parabola's vertex, Δχ² against the 95.45 % χ² quantile of the difference and the turns equal widths free (6.18 for an ellipsoid's pair or an elliptic section, 8.02 for a spheroid). The review found the linear z of such pairs wrong away from 0 too, and by crystal: equal pairs told apart in 13 of 62 draws on rutile and ZnO (rutile ellipsoids 8 of 28, mean z² 3.0; elliptic cylinders 4 of 12), none of 40 on SrTiO₃ (mean z² 0.5, and a 6 × 12 × 25 nm ellipsoid's 12 and 25 nm axes grouped at z 1.4). The profile: 6 of 62 (4 of 28, 1 of 12, ZnO 1 of 12, needles 0 of 10), corundum 0 of 10, SrTiO₃ 0 of 20 (mean z² 0.8), the 12 and 25 nm axes apart (2.2); 20 iterations instead of 4 moved z by 0.02 at most (7 draws). So every ball pair and elliptic section now goes by the profile; the group's width and esd stay the linear ones where the smaller width's esd is under it (mean (error/esd)² 0.95 over the 28 rutile ellipsoids, 1.7 from the profile's curvature), the profile's elsewhere (16 common widths of the 150 nm plates 0.97 esd rms from the truth; with the rest held, up to 14 esd). Held on the lattice the fit has no turn free: 1 degree (4), not 6.18 or 8.02. Faces keep the full covariance (a profile had told a box's equal edges apart at 3.2 against 2.0, its mean 3.3 esd off). 2 of the 9 on 0 apart (z 2.1, 2.8). The spheroid plate stays oblate (esd 837°, z 86); the disc's diameter reads "over 336 nm". Cost: 0.5–10 s more per reading under load, beside 3–60 s for the angular esds | adaptive |
| Told apart (2σ) | 43 pairs of equal widths both above 0: boxes 12 × 12 × 3 and 150 × 150 × 4 nm (SrTiO₃), 120 × 120 × 4 nm (rutile); elliptic cylinders 15 × 15 × 4 nm (ZnO) and 150 × 150 × 4 nm; ellipsoids. Review: boxes with two equal edges, 12 × 12 × 3 nm on SrTiO₃ (24 draws), rutile (12), ZnO (14), corundum (14), Pnma (10), and 5 × 5 × 20 nm rutile needles (12) | The other agent: 3 of 43 told apart, against 2.0 expected; mean z² 0.6 on the 12 nm boxes. The review: on the non-cubic boxes z² averaged 1.9 (rutile 2.0, ZnO 1.7, corundum 1.6, Pnma 3.1, needles 1.4), as for two degrees of freedom, and z > 2 told 9 of 62 apart (15 %): equal edges leave the turn about the third edge only the square's fourfold to act through, so the pair behaves as a ball's. The 95.45 % level kept, now set against the χ² quantile of the degrees of freedom equal widths take away where the fit has the turns free (box edges, ellipsoid pairs, elliptic sections 6.18, z_lin > 2.49; a spheroid 8.02; a cylinder or a held body 4): 3 of 62 (4.8 %). SrTiO₃ boxes stay small (mean z² 0.5, 0 of 24). On the 32 plates the boxes' z is 0.2–1.8 (one box had been apart at 2.3). The thin dimension against the others: z 12–200 | changed |
| Unbounded dimension | 4 nm discs of diameter 200 nm (6 draws) and unbounded (3 draws), 20 000 counts; set esds 0.001–5° | Unbounded: "over 335–342 nm". 200 nm: 151–225 nm, within 1.9 esd. From 1 nm: at σ_W = 1° "over 4 nm" beside a 4 nm thickness, at 5° "unbounded". A ball's axis on 0 (linear esd 20–840°) reads "unbounded" without a limit, as its esd says nothing there | kept |

Now a group counts as its mean, as the v420 ellipsoid's did: 4 of 32, the pairs still told apart (3 of 32 with the review's quantile for box pairs). ½ and 20 % kept: labels, with the dimensions beside them (Zingg's ⅔ would be as arbitrary) | changed |

Frames off the lattice are refused by the χ² test, not by the snap. In cubic cells every axis has a label within 10°; in others an axis can be 11–20° from any direction of indices ≤ 4 (it then gets the nearest, with its angle) | kept |

| turnEsds trial turns | not retested (tested-synthetic); the coverage below uses them | — | kept |

The other 2 axes, a box's in-plane edges 11° and 31° off, lie in a second valley (3–9 esd), which no combination covers | kept |

**v427 tests.** Synthetic patterns from the engine (Cu Kα1/Kα2 on a laboratory profile, a high-resolution Kα1 profile, Mo; SrTiO₃, rutile, anatase, ZnO, corundum, quartz, YAG, a Pnma and a monoclinic cell; Lorentzian sizes and strains, Stephens strain, WPPM spheres and log-normal sizes; 600 to 100 000 counts; steps 0.005–0.02°), Poisson noise, read by `measureWidths` with the truth or a refined isotropic state; z against the truth's own widths. Scripts in `scratchpad/v427/B7/`. Over seven structures read with the truth (`t-base`, v426 against v427, 2 seeds) the rows the tests use rise from 8–23 to 15–27 a pattern (Pnma 8 → 15: the overlap share net of the straight line), their z mean −0.27 to +0.34 and sd 0.78–1.06 (v426: −0.07 to +0.43, sd 0.78–1.39); the model's own widths come within 0.14 % of the analytic ones (v426: up to 4.5 % on Pnma, 5.6 % on quartz, merged rows); and weak rows whose fit landed off their position, or past the width bounds off their intensity, are now unread (Pnma 58 → 169 of 354 rows, weak 217 → 107; after the review, the rows used unchanged). The review's scripts are in `scratchpad/v427/B7/rev/`.

| Constant | Test | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| Isolation model | Stephens strain (ε² ±40 % by direction) read by an isotropic refinement, five structures × 3 seeds, 20 000 counts (`t-overlap`); the same with an isotropic truth, and with broad lines (8 nm). Review: a SrTiO₃ disc 60 × 4 nm (20 000 counts), the cylinder kept by `autoRefine`, the data isolated with the isotropic refinement, the kept shape or the truth, each row against the truth's own curve read on the same window with the same fit (`t-isol4`); two-phase samples (`t-multi`, see Overlap flag) | The isotropic neighbours' error enters only where they overlap: rows holding under 5 % of others read z rms 1.0, 5–30 % 1.6–1.9, more 1.8–2.7; with the model right, 0.7–1.15 at any share. Review: on the disc, isotropic isolation z −0.34 (rms 0.77), the kept shape's +0.13 (0.75), the truth's +0.04 (0.70); the kept shape's score 0.33 and 0.41. (Against the truth read on its own window the rows had seemed 2–7 % narrow: a non-Voigt peak, the family's members of different widths, reads differently on another window; data and models are always read on the same one.) | kept: a test of a shape must not read the data through it; its cost is confined to overlapped rows, which the overlap rule drops when the isotropic model misfits, phase by phase (review) |
| Coincident reflections | noise-free patterns of eight structures, merged rows against the analytic width; doublets 0.1–0.5 FWHM apart with the model's intensity ratio right or 2:1 off (`t-coinc`, `t-coinc2`) | One line shape at the group's position read two reflections 0.05–0.1 H apart 1.2 % wide, 0.15–0.2 H apart 3.6 % (up to 6.9 %); each at its own position and share: within 0.04 %. With the ratio 2:1 off: 0.2–0.4 % at 0.1 H, 0.9–1.6 % at 0.2 H, 3–4.5 % at 0.3 H | changed: the members are fitted at their own positions by their shares of the intensity, the row's 2θ their centroid; 0.2·min H kept (a 2:1 error stays under 1.6 %) |
| Window | — | — | already v426 (now from the width found when it is off by 2×, see Peak identity) |

| Overlap flag | rows binned by the others' share across the top, net of the straight line the fit's background takes up (`t-overlap`): as above, and Stephens strain with broad lines (3 structures × 3 seeds) and at 5000 counts (3 × 2); the test of the model (χ² of the rows up to 0.3 against the model's widths) on 80 isotropic patterns: six structures on a laboratory and a high-resolution profile and on Mo Kα, WPPM spheres, log-normal sizes, a monoclinic and a Pnma cell, 5000 and 100 000 counts (`t-isoright`). Review: an isotropic phase beside a Stephens-strained or an isotropic one, four pairs of structures × 3 or 2 seeds, 20 000 counts (`t-multi`); log-normal sizes (σ 0.6) or an instrument 30 % broader in its Gaussian part than the standard's, refined with the default Lorentzian, six structures, 5000 and 100 000 counts (`t-mismatch`) | Isotropic: z rms 0.7–1.15 at every share up to over 2. Stephens: see above. The model test, each row counting at most 3 esd: isotropic p ≥ 0.014 (0 of 80 judged anisotropic; uncapped, one YAG pattern through one weak row read on a noise spike, z −6.5); Stephens p ≤ 7.7·10⁻⁷ in all 30. Those then use their clear rows: z rms 0.7–1.5, 5–18 rows; broad rutile and ZnO keep 2, where at 0.3 their 13–14 rows read z rms 1.6–3.0 (judged on the clear rows alone, these had passed by default, under three rows). Review: with the limit set by the row's own phase, the isotropic phase read z rms 1.3–1.6 under 5–30 % of the strained phase's reflections (0.85–1.0 beside an isotropic one) and its WH p fell to 2·10⁻⁴–2·10⁻² in 5 of 12 patterns (0.2–0.98); with the strained phase's reflections held to 0.05 under every peak it keeps half its rows, p ≥ 1.9·10⁻³. A line shape the model lacks fails the test as well: log-normal 11 of 12 (rows at 5–30 % z rms 1.15–1.5, under 5 % 1.07), the broader instrument 1 of 12 | adaptive: 0.3 for the reflections of a phase whose isotropic model's widths predict those read (capped χ², p ≥ 10⁻³), 0.05 for those of a phase whose do not, under every peak of any phase (review: set by the row's own phase before); the share net of the line (a strong neighbour's far tail held 5 % of a weak broad peak's top) |

With v426's identity rule 7 of 96 rows at area/esd 5–7 had read noise spikes 4–6 times too narrow, the centre held at the bound; now unread (see Peak identity) | kept |

Review: unmodelled humps (FWHM 1.5–4°, 3–12 % of the strongest peak) under four structures × 3 seeds, the background refined (`t-hump2`); the area found against the model's intensity on isotropic, 8× broad and textured patterns (`t-ai`) | Width bound: up to 3× within 1 %, 4× 2–5 % narrow, 6× 34 % narrow (held at 4·H0 on a window a third of the peak); centre: beyond 0.5 H0 held at the bound, 1–13 % off. With the re-fitted window and the centre judged after the fit, a pattern 8× broader than its model: SrTiO₃ 29 of 40 rows used at −6.6 % (v426: 4 of 80 at −51 %), ZnO 22 of 56 at −4.7 % (v426: 12 of 112, −48 %; final code, 2 seeds); noise spikes unread. Gated by the width's esds, the re-fit left 10 of 198 weak rows (4–7 esd) 3–6 esd narrow, against 1: the second look is what shows a spike off its reflection. Review: with the bounds following the passes, a weak reflection on an unmodelled hump (FWHM 3.5°, 7 % of the strongest peak) was read 4.4× as broad as its model, esd 5 %, z +15.5, with 12 times its intensity: the one row past 5 esd of 269 used, z rms 1.54 (v426: 274, 1.22). With the intensity check past the bounds: 268 rows, z rms 1.21, none past 5. Read right, the area is 0.73–1.37 times the model's intensity (252 rows); in patterns 8× broader than their model 138 of the 148 rows past 4× keep it within 3×; an unmodelled texture (r 0.5–0.7) spreads it to 0.2–34 but put no row past the width bounds. A plain 0.25–4× verdict instead had left 61 of 80 SrTiO₃ rows of the 8× pattern unread | changed: the search keeps the centre within H0 and the width within 0.25–4·H0; a width off by more than 2× is fitted again on a window from it (up to three passes, a pass that finds nothing keeping the previous fit); a centre more than half the found FWHM off leaves the row unread; (review) so does a width past 0.25–4·H0 whose area is not within a factor 3 of the model's intensity (`ID_INTENSITY`) |

Review: an isotropic phase beside a Stephens-strained one (`t-multi`) was called anisotropic in 1 of 12 (p 2.2·10⁻⁴) through the other's reflections taken off its peaks; none with the overlap limit by phase (p ≥ 1.9·10⁻³) | kept: the isotropic patterns sit far inside it; the misses are misread rows, not the threshold |

**v429.** The overlap flag's 5 % (above) dropped the (111) of the user's 36D and 37D once the texture was refined: SrTiO₃ of lines 1.3° wide, the far Lorentzian tails of (110) and (200), 7.5° and 6.4° away, hold 7.2–7.3 % of its top net of the straight line, and the isotropic model misfits there (the broadening is anisotropic, the very signal). Williamson–Hall then read p 0.014 and 0.024 against v426's 5·10⁻³⁸ and 6·10⁻³¹. A share counts a smooth far tail as it counts a neighbour under the top, and does not scale with the counts. The 5 % is replaced by what the misfitting phase's reflections can do to the row's width (`biasOff`, RIETVELD.md §12.3): each off by its own measured misfit where its row is read (signed, its esd in quadrature), else by the phase's misfit beyond the esds τ (Paule–Mandel, in quadrature), its pseudo-Voigt widened by that much and taken through the row's own fit to first order (Gauss–Newton from the optimum, the fit's line included). Up to half an esd the row is used as it is, to 2 esd with its esds widened by the bias in quadrature, past 2 esd it is left out. The 30 % share and the model test (capped χ², p ≥ 10⁻³) are unchanged. Synthetic patterns from the engine, z against the truth's own-curve widths, the v427 sets (`t-overlap` as `t-ov`, `rev/t-multi` as `t-mp`, `rev/t-mismatch` as `t-ln`, `t-isoright`) and more; scripts and outputs in `scratchpad/v429/W/` (`sum.mjs` reads the dumps under v428's rule and the first one, `run-final.sh` runs them) and `scratchpad/v429/W/R/` (the review: `sum3.mjs` reads them under v428's rule, the first and the final, `calib.mjs` bins rows by the bias, `run-R.sh`, `run-L.sh`, `run-F.sh` the final code).

The first rule left a row out past half an esd, with the misfit of unread reflections the plain rms of e² − s². The review kept the bias and changed what follows from it: the plain rms read 0 on 4 of 78 synthetic phases judged off and on 33A without its (111), so τ; and dropping every row past half an esd lost Williamson–Hall on discs and spheroids at 5000 counts, which the 5 % had called anisotropic, so rows from half an esd to 2 are used with their esds widened. Columns: rows used, z rms, past 3 esd; Williamson–Hall.

**v427 tests.** Most rows here format or word what the refinement found; they were checked on synthetic values over the general range (`t-fmt`, `t-misc`). The ones that move a number (the standard's B, the weight-fraction esd, the log-normal warning, the ΔBIC bands) were tested on synthetic patterns. Scripts in `scratchpad/v427/B7/`.

| Constant | Test | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| Esd notation | 200 000 random (value, esd) pairs, esd 10⁻¹²–10¹², \|value\| 10⁻¹²–10¹⁴, both signs; worker against card on 20 000 | The esd shown within its one- or two-digit rounding (20 % at most, as 0.025 → (3)), the value at the esd's last digit; worker and card agree on all. An esd under 10⁻¹⁰⁰ threw (toFixed takes at most 100 decimals) and failed the whole summary | kept (IUCr convention); an esd under 10⁻¹⁵ of the value, below a double's precision, now prints the value alone |
| Numerical propagation step | forward difference with h = max(10⁻⁷, 10⁻⁶·\|p\|) against a Richardson central difference, on the log-normal median and number mean (σ 0–1.2), a monoclinic V, an rms strain with clipping, B_eq from U^ij (`t-misc`) | Error in the propagated esd at most 7·10⁻⁶ of it | kept |
| Wide log-normal warning | log-normal WPPM SrTiO₃ (D_V 20 nm, 8000 counts), σ 0.5, 0.8 and 1.1, 2 seeds each, by `autoRefine`, with and without an unmodelled amorphous hump (FWHM 12°, 40 % of the background) (`t-lognorm`) | Without the hump σ and the median within 1.1 esd at every σ. With it, D_V within 0.6 % but the median −3.2 %, −8.0 % and −18.4 % (z −2.0, −3.1, −3.8): the warning's claim holds, the more the wider (d ln median/dσ = −7σ). Its own criterion, tails heavier than the Lorentzian's (⟨L⟩_A/⟨L⟩_V = (8/9)e^{−σ²} < 0.5), starts at 0.7585, so 0.76–0.8 went unwarned | derived: σ ≥ √ln(16/9) = 0.7585 |
| Stephens planes reported | `lowIndexPlanes` on cubic, tetragonal, hexagonal, trigonal, orthorhombic, monoclinic and triclinic phases | One plane per Laue family: 3, 5, 5, 6, 7, 7, 7 planes | kept |
| "Not told from no texture" | — | \|r − 1\| < 2 esd as written; a tooltip | kept (2σ convention) |
| Angle display | the worker's format against the 3D view's on 10⁵ values 10⁻⁴–100° | Identical on all (v426 unified them) | kept |
| Solid frame for the view | — | The directions across a body of revolution are perpendicular to its axis by construction, so \|x0\| > 10⁻⁶ only excludes a degenerate input; 0.999 (2.6°) picks the frame axis that is the solid's axis, equal to rounding | kept (guards) |
| ΔBIC reading bands | SrTiO₃ drawn from a spheroid or a cylinder (30 × 8 nm, tilted 23°, 8000 counts), each refined as a spheroid and as a cylinder, 2 seeds (`t-dbic`) | Wrong solid's ΔBIC less the right one's: −1.0, +7.6 (spheroid drawn), +5.2, +1.8 (cylinder drawn): the right solid ahead in 3 of 4, by 1.8–7.6, the wrong one once by 1.0; never 10 | kept: under 10 is within what noise and the search give between solids of as many parameters (the right one led by −1 to +8), and Kass & Raftery's "very strong" starts at 10; the upper band was not reached by these solids |
| Weight fraction shown | — | One detected phase is 100 % by definition | kept |
| Weight-fraction esd | two-phase patterns, 20 000 counts, refined from the truth, 25 seeds (rutile + anatase 50 %, ZnO + corundum 30 %) and 8 seeds with ΔB refined or a 5 % minority (`t-wf`) | Pull of W: mean −0.15 to +0.76, rms 0.99 and 1.22 (25 seeds), 1.11–1.50 (8 seeds); the cell's relative esd is two orders below the scale's (10⁻⁵ against 10⁻³) | kept (the scales' covariance from the full refinement carries the B's and the profile's correlations; the scatter's 1.0–1.6 times the esd is the refinement's covariance, not the propagation) |
| No broadening | `sizeStrain` at Y_s = X_s = 0 | D = ∞, ε = 0; the card writes "no measurable broadening" | kept |
| Built-in LaB6: cell | — | NIST SRM 660c certificate, 4.156826 Å at 22.5 °C | kept (literature) |
| Built-in LaB6: B | synthetic LaB6 standards (40 000 counts, 15–145°, 2 seeds) drawn with B(La) 0.15–0.6 and B(B) 0.2–0.6 Å², refined on the standard's path with the card's CIF (`t-lab6`, `t-lab6b`) | Held at 0.25/0.35: a B 0.1 Å² low moved X by −10 esd and Y by +8, the FWHM at 80° by +0.7 %; 0.2 Å² high, X +21 esd, FWHM −1.4 %; (0.6, 0.2) −2.4 %. With one ΔB refined: every profile parameter within 1 esd of the truth, the FWHM within 0.1 % (0.27 % at 130° for the mixed case), the esds 10 % wider; ΔB found −0.094 and +0.206 for −0.10 and +0.20/+0.25. Review (`t-lab6r`, 2–3 seeds): an intensity fall no B models (surface roughness, Suortti's form, p 0.3, q 0.4) on the same standards, held B (right, or 0.1 Å² high): X −23 to −28 esd, Y +18 to +21, FWHM at 80° +1.6 to +2.1 %; one ΔB takes it up (−0.23 and −0.32 Å²; −0.03 where the held B's were 0.2 Å² low and had partly offset it, X −3.7 esd): X within 0.5 esd, FWHM within 0.2 % in all three. A short scan (20–80°, 5000 counts): held or refined alike in range (FWHM at 30° and 80° within 0.5–0.7 %, X within 2.4 esd held, 0.1 refined), the esd of X 18 % wider | adaptive: one ΔB refined on the standard from 0.25/0.35 |
| Built-in LaB6: x(B) | x 0.1975 against 0.1992 in the truth | Profile within 0.2 esd of the x = 0.1992 run, FWHM within 0.02 % | kept (literature) |
| What the standard passes | — | {U, V, W, X, Y, asym, zero} with λ1 and the radius since v426 | kept |
| Option defaults per phase | — | The simplest model; each option is the user's to choose | kept |
| Texture choices | — | `auto` takes the best of the low-index axes (index ≤ 1); (012) and the like are not offered | kept |
| Planar-fault kinds | each spec built on a structure where it is a fault (Si, ZnO, SrTiO₃) and on one where it is a translation (NaCl, F-centred) | Tables of 4, 1, 3, 3, 6 orientations; {111}½⟨110⟩ on NaCl refused with the engine's warning (h·R an integer for every reflection) | kept |
| Texture and fault spec grammar | '1 1 1 : 1/6 <1 1 2>', '12-1:1/2<110>', '111:1/6<1120>', '000:1/2<110>' | The first two parsed, the last two refused | kept |
| Default λ for ticks | — | The engine's CU | kept |
| θ cap of the tick list | a scan to 180° | d_min = λ/(2 sin 89.9°), 1.5·10⁻⁶ above λ/2 | kept (guard) |
| Legacy migrations | — | Paths read; no effect on new data | kept |
| CSV decimals | values of 10⁻⁹–10³ through the export | At fixed decimals an esd of 3·10⁻⁷ read 0.00000030 (eight decimals), a Δd* esd of 10⁻⁶ 0.000001, a rate of 4·10⁻⁴ cps 0.000 | changed: the module's `fmtData`, as the EPR export: a value too small for its decimals keeps three significant digits or more; other values as before |
| Card display | WH p-values 10⁻³²⁰–1; model widths 10⁻⁴–2°; the WH plot's range (v426) | p from 10⁻⁴ to 10⁻³, where the verdict turns, read "0.000"; p under 10⁻³⁰⁶ "0e+0"; a model width under 0.0005° "0.000" | changed: p with two significant digits, one below 10⁻³, "< 1e-300" under it; model widths with three significant digits under 0.1°; an overlapped row's tooltip gives the share and the limit that applied (30 %, or 5 % for the reflections of a phase whose isotropic model does not predict its widths), and a note under that phase says so (§3.2.12); the rest kept |
